src/lib/utils.ts:export const BDTshort = (n: number | string) => {
src/lib/utils.ts:  const v = Number(n || 0);
src/lib/utils.ts:  if (v < 0) return "-" + BDTshort(-v);
src/lib/utils.ts:  if (v >= 10000000) return "৳" + (v / 10000000).toFixed(1) + "Cr";
src/lib/utils.ts:  if (v >= 100000) return "৳" + (v / 100000).toFixed(v % 100000 === 0 ? 0 : 1) + "L";
src/lib/utils.ts:  if (v >= 1000) return "৳" + (v / 1000).toFixed(v % 1000 === 0 ? 0 : 1) + "K";
src/lib/utils.ts:  return "৳" + v;

src/lib/utils.ts:export const numberToWords = (num: number): string => {

src/lib/utils.ts:  const a = ['', 'One ', 'Two ', 'Three ', 'Four ', 'Five ', 'Six ', 'Seven ', 'Eight ', 'Nine ', 'Ten ', 'Eleven ', 'Twelve ', 'Thirteen ', 'Fourteen ', 'Fifteen ', 'Sixteen ', 'Seventeen ', 'Eighteen ', 'Nineteen '];

src/lib/utils.ts:  const nStr = num.toString();

src/lib/i18n.tsx:
src/lib/i18n.tsx:export const LanguageProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Language>('bn');
src/lib/i18n.tsx:
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  useEffect(() => {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    const savedLang = localStorage.getItem('app_lang') as Language;
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    if (savedLang && (savedLang === 'bn' || savedLang === 'en')) {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:      setLangState(savedLang);
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    }
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  }, []);
src/lib/i18n.tsx:
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  const setLang = (newLang: Language) => {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    setLangState(newLang);
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    localStorage.setItem('app_lang', newLang);
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  };
src/lib/i18n.tsx:
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  const t = (key: string, params?: Record<string, any>): string => {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    let text = translations[lang][key] || translations['bn'][key] || key;
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    if (params) {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:      Object.entries(params).forEach(([k, v]) => {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:        text = text.replace(`{{${k}}}`, String(v));
src/lib/i18n.tsx:      });
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    }
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    return text;
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  };
src/lib/i18n.tsx:
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  return (
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ lang, setLang, t }}>
src/lib/i18n.tsx:      {children}
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    </LanguageContext.Provider>
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  );
src/lib/i18n.tsx:};
src/lib/i18n.tsx:
src/lib/i18n.tsx:export const useLanguage = () => {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  const context = useContext(LanguageContext);
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  if (context === undefined) {
src/lib/i18n.tsx:    throw new Error('useLanguage must be used within a LanguageProvider');
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  }
src/lib/i18n.tsx:  return context;
src/lib/i18n.tsx:};

src/App.tsx:function FinancialSummary({ projects, clients, instDefs, payments, expenses, plans }: any) {
src/App.tsx:  const { t } = useLanguage();
src/App.tsx:  const approvedPays = payments.filter((p: any) => p.status === "approved");
src/App.tsx:  const pendingPays = payments.filter((p: any) => p.status === "pending");
src/App.tsx:  const totalExpected = clients.reduce((s: number, c: any) => {
src/App.tsx:    const prjPlans = plans.filter((p: any) => p.projectId === c.projectId);
src/App.tsx:    // If client has assignments, use them. Otherwise use the first plan found for the project.
src/App.tsx:    const assignments = c.planAssignments || [];
src/App.tsx:    if (assignments.length > 0) {
src/App.tsx:      return s + assignments.reduce((as: number, pa: any) => {
src/App.tsx:        const pDefs = instDefs.filter((d: any) => d.planId === pa.planId);
src/App.tsx:        return as + (pDefs.reduce((ds: number, d: any) => ds + d.targetAmount, 0) * pa.shareCount);
src/App.tsx:      }, 0);
src/App.tsx:    } else {
src/App.tsx:      // Fallback to first plan if no assignment
src/App.tsx:      const firstPlan = prjPlans[0];
src/App.tsx:      if (!firstPlan) return s;
src/App.tsx:      const pDefs = instDefs.filter((d: any) => d.planId === firstPlan.id);
src/App.tsx:      return s + (pDefs.reduce((ds: number, d: any) => ds + d.targetAmount, 0) * (c.shareCount || 1));
src/App.tsx:    }
src/App.tsx:  }, 0);
src/App.tsx:  const totalCollected = approvedPays.reduce((s: number, p: any) => s + p.amount, 0);
src/App.tsx:  const totalExpenses = expenses.reduce((s: number, e: any) => s + e.amount, 0);
src/App.tsx:  const totalPending = pendingPays.reduce((s: number, p: any) => s + p.amount, 0);
src/App.tsx:  const totalDue = Math.max(0, totalExpected - totalCollected);
src/App.tsx:  const netProfit = totalCollected - totalExpenses;
src/App.tsx:  const collectPct = totalExpected > 0 ? Math.round((totalCollected / totalExpected) * 100) : 0;
src/App.tsx:  
src/App.tsx:  return (
src/App.tsx:    <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} className="pb-20">
src/App.tsx:      <div className="mb-4">
src/App.tsx:        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
src/App.tsx:          <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center">

firestore.rules:      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'name', 'username', 'password', 'role', 'isTemp']) &&
firestore.rules:             data.keys().hasOnly(['id', 'name', 'username', 'password', 'role', 'isTemp']) &&
firestore.rules:             data.name is string && data.name.size() > 0 && data.name.size() < 100 &&
firestore.rules:             data.username is string && data.username.size() > 0 && data.username.size() < 50 &&
firestore.rules:             data.password is string && data.password.size() > 0 && data.password.size() < 100 &&
firestore.rules:             data.role in ['superadmin', 'admin'] &&
firestore.rules:             data.isTemp is bool;
firestore.rules:    }

firestore.rules:             data.keys().hasOnly(['id', 'name', 'description']) &&
firestore.rules:             data.name is string && data.name.size() > 0 && data.name.size() < 100 &&
firestore.rules:             (!('description' in data) || (data.description is string && data.description.size() < 1000));
firestore.rules:    }
firestore.rules:

firestore.rules:             data.keys().hasOnly(['id', 'projectId', 'name']) &&
firestore.rules:             data.name is string && data.name.size() > 0 && data.name.size() < 100;
firestore.rules:    }
firestore.rules:
firestore.rules:    function isValidClient(data) {
firestore.rules:      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'projectId', 'name']) &&
firestore.rules:             data.keys().hasOnly(['id', 'projectId', 'name', 'fatherHusband', 'birthDate', 'phone', 'email', 'nid', 'plot', 'totalAmount', 'shareCount', 'password', 'photo', 'remarks', 'planAssignments', '_row']) &&
firestore.rules:             data.name is string && data.name.size() > 0 && data.name.size() < 100 &&
firestore.rules:             (!('phone' in data) || (data.phone is string && data.phone.size() < 20)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('fatherHusband' in data) || (data.fatherHusband is string && data.fatherHusband.size() < 100)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('birthDate' in data) || (data.birthDate is string && data.birthDate.size() < 20)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('email' in data) || isValidEmail(data.email)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('nid' in data) || (data.nid is string && data.nid.size() < 50)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('plot' in data) || (data.plot is string && data.plot.size() < 100)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('password' in data) || (data.password is string && data.password.size() < 100)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('photo' in data) || (data.photo is string && data.photo.size() < 1000000)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('remarks' in data) || (data.remarks is string && data.remarks.size() < 1000)) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('totalAmount' in data) || isPositive('totalAmount')) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('shareCount' in data) || isPositive('shareCount')) &&
firestore.rules:             (!('planAssignments' in data) || (data.planAssignments is list && data.planAssignments.size() < 10));
